7. The message to AMCHALK-5 follows:

M.

We arranged to meet "PUSHKIN"’ recently but
you apparently called the mecting off. We can
understand your hesitancy to mect with us in that
place and at that time. We do, however, wish to
meet with you anytime, any place of mutual agree-
ment via any secure channel of your personal
confidence, As you can sec, in this instance,
we have selected an officer whose loyalty to
you is unquestioned. We intended to tell
“PUSHIKIN' of possible pitfalls in your path,

We still wish you to know of these dangers.

We realize that you are a true revolutionary

in every sense of the word, We have no

quarrel with you as a true revolutionary nor
with the system that has established itself
over the years, be will not countenance nor

do we belicve that you will countenance continued
foreign intervention in your system wherein
lies the major danger to your futre. We would
also like to make you an offer which would be
difficult for you to refuse. To contact one

of our reliable officers concerning this
opening of a two-way channel of communications,
contact or have your selected man contact DIAZ
in which you shoved some interest in 1967 and ~

he will then contact us. He will then relay : ;

the time and mecting date to us, and we will
then open our dialogue using only trusted

personne],
Greetings,

L.

8. I believe that cur chances for success in this
operation are reasonably good for the following re
(a) AMCHALK-5 is sufficiently courageous to meet
in the Cuban Ambassador's office in Madrid, if necessary;

(b) AMWOLF-1 is a trusted colleague and follower of
AMGHOST-1. For this reason and in the belief that AMGHOST-1
was awaiting the message, he would find a way to deliver the
message uncpened to AMGHOST - 1; (c) ANGHCST-1 from all
available reporting, back round material, SI traffic, we have
reason to believe that AMCHOST-1 is not held in the confidence
of the Seviets nor does he respect their presence in Cuba,

a presence which can only be interpreted as promising
difficulties for AMGHOST-1; und (d) Bona fides for the
originator are contained in a copy of a letter from AMGHOST-
to her relatives which she signs with the name "PUSHKIN."

the black marketeers referred to may be on the verge of
@nother trip to Hiami, It might be interesting from the
J8ROD atandpoint to "piggyback" that channel and send in
am incriminating package to a JMROD target, AEHCHALE-3
@s the source of tho package is alre2zdy well identified
through the ASGHOST operation as affiliated with RVRCCE,

There are the two possibilities that the couriers thomselves

would open the package, inspect tho contents and turn the
20 package over to the DSE or that they would deliver
it unopened to the target. In the latter case the target
might oither turn it over to the DSB, destroy it, or make
use of the contents for his own benefit, Content of the
package would of course have to be tailored to the target
but it might purport to be an agent re-supply cache,
Rany possible avenues rould have to be explored; for the
moment we would appreciate consideration of the proposal
by the Desk and JEROD staff, One of the first items to
be resolved is whether or not there is an inherent "blow
back" feature of tho gituation which could cause too much
embarransrent if exposed to press play. Specifically,
is there & cage for an allegation that RYROCK was using

the international orgenization with which tho “courtiers”
ere affiliated for "subversive" purposes? Tha answer to
that question probably lies aomewhere in betweon what tho
eontents of the package consists of and to what lengths
the Cubans would bo willing to go to “burn” the “leyal

eonrads” couriers.
